better: sm 142, webrtc 105, tie 2613 (of the metrics shown)

Metrics measured differently for libwebrtc:
  audio.jb_delay_p50_ms / p95: Percentiles of the jitter buffer delay averaged over each 10 ms of playout (jitterBufferDelay / jitterBufferEmittedCount), as the test bench reads its own
  audio.net_delay_p95_ms: Measured from the capture time of the packet's first sample, (ts - first ts) / 48 ms, as the test bench measures from its RTP timestamp; the first packet is the frame of the 10 ms blocks captured at 0 and 10 ms
  video.residual_loss_pct / repair.nack_recovered / repair.overhead_pct: testbench::RepairMeter, the rule and events the test bench uses (#509). FEC recoveries come from libwebrtc's UlpfecReceiver run on the same RED arrivals as the receiver's (VideoReceiveStream does not expose them)
  video.undecodable_frames: VideoReceiveStream frames_dropped
  video.delay_*: Each frame's capture and encoded times from the encoder model, the send time of its last media packet at the send transport, the arrival of its last media or RTX packet on the forward link, and its render time, as the test bench measures them (#510)
  audio.time_to_first_ms: Arrival of the first audio packet after which NetEq holds audio (jitter_buffer_ms or total_samples_received above 0), read right after the packet is delivered (#510)
  repair.fec_recovered: num_recovered_packets of that UlpfecReceiver (#509)
  net.<kind>_kbps / _loss_pct: Counted at the forward link by SSRC: audio, video media, RTX with a payload, FEC (ULPFEC in RED on the video SSRC), and padding (a packet with no payload after RTP padding is removed, including RTX with only its sequence number)
  cc.goodput_kbps: Each media sequence number counts once, as original or RTX (#329): excludes padding-only packets, RTX payload padding and RTX copies of packets that had already arrived
  sync.av_offset_*: The audio playout point is AudioReceiveStream's Syncable::GetPlayoutRtpTimestamp, the input libwebrtc's A/V sync uses, read after each 10 ms pull (the test bench reads its jitter buffer's playout timestamp right after the pull)
